Strategy rules (updated Nov 18, 2026)

From Nov 18, 2026: pools are ranked and entered on a sustained APY, the lower of the 7-day and 30-day mean from DefiLlama's history (current APY × 0.7 if no history), not today's rate. Rebalances compare sustained APY on both sides and still need +3.0 pp and a 14-day payback on simulated gas. No held pool may exceed 30% of the book: the excess is trimmed only if the move pays back within 14 days, or always if the pool passes 40%; otherwise it is flagged. Reward tokens are sold at most once every 24 hours, paying claim gas plus a 0.3% swap cost; unsold rewards count in the total only net of that cost. Gas and cost modelling are otherwise unchanged.

Effective APY uses organic apyBase plus a 12% haircut on reward APY (reward APY is marked volatile when present). This is a very high-risk book targeting 25%+ blended APY. Simulated gas is deducted (about $0.50 on L2s, $3.00 on Ethereum). Entry/move costs currently sitting in open positions: $25.50. Max 25% per pool at entry, 30% hold cap, 5–7 pools. TVL floor $1M (prefer $5M+). Rebalance if a pool beats a sleeve by 3.0 pp and move costs break even within 14 days.

Value over time

Chart times are Mountain Time (America/Edmonton). The dashed line is the $10,000.00 starting capital. Early P&L is usually slightly negative because simulated gas is paid up front.
